# Graph Report - graphify  (2026-10-10)

## Corpus Check
- Large corpus: 937 files · ~1,649,964 words. Semantic extraction will be expensive (many Claude tokens). Consider running on a subfolder.

## Summary
- 16936 nodes · 34941 edges · 859 communities (792 shown, 67 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 95% EXTRACTED · 5% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1764 edges (avg confidence: 0.86)
- Token cost: 3,670,862 input · 0 output
